# Agents · M · part 15

930 entries, montaj-grafika to multi-agent-systems-java. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **montaj-grafika**: Graphics department of the montage studio. Use when you have the meaning breakdown, the clean speech cut and the inserts: assembles the reel's spec.json in the chosen style — layers over a continuous shot, speaker windows, counters, grids, lists, echo word, final call to action. Places everything off the face and off the caption strip, and ties each appearance to words. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/montaj-grafika.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-kachestvo**: Quality department of the montage studio. Use before montage on every source file: measures noise, skin-tone balance, face exposure, sharpness and sound, decides what to fix, and fixes only what went past the threshold. Separates fixing defects from the color look of a style. Returns kachestvo.json and the corrected files. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/montaj-kachestvo.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-kachestvo**: Отдел качества монтажной студии. Использовать до монтажа на каждом исходнике: меряет шум, баланс по тону кожи, экспозицию лица, резкость и звук, решает, что чинить, и чинит только то, что вышло за порог. Отделяет починку брака от цветового вида стиля. Отдаёт kachestvo.json и исправленные файлы. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/docs/ru/agents/montaj-kachestvo.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-rezka**: Cutting department of the montage studio. Use after the meaning breakdown: cuts out pauses, failed takes, stretches of reading from notes and the off-screen prompter voice, and assembles a continuous clean speech cut with soft cuts. Decides by meaning where to cut and where to leave a pause for weight. Separates the prompter voice with the script `speechsplit.py`, cuts pauses with `jumpcut.py`. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/montaj-rezka.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-rezka**: Отдел резки монтажной студии. Использовать после смыслового разбора: вырезает паузы, неудачные дубли, куски чтения с листа и закадровую подсказку, собирает непрерывный чистовик речи с мягкими стыками. Решает по смыслу, где резать, а где оставить паузу для веса. Подсказку отделяет скриптом `speechsplit.py`, паузы режет `jumpcut.py`. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/docs/ru/agents/montaj-rezka.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-sborka**: Отдел сборки монтажной студии. Использовать последним: проверяет спеку, собирает ролик, снимает лист ключевых кадров, прогоняет qc.py и отдаёт всё критикам кадра и удержания. Знает обходы, когда рендер заблокирован системой. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/docs/ru/agents/montaj-sborka.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-sborka**: Assembly department of the montage studio. Use last: checks the spec, assembles the video, takes a keyframe sheet, runs qc.py and hands the result to the montage director (there are no critics). Knows the workarounds when rendering is blocked by the system. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/montaj-sborka.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-smysl**: Смысловой отдел монтажной студии. Использовать первым шагом монтажа любого ролика: расшифровывает речь, понимает тему, посыл, аудиторию и эмоцию, режет речь на смысловые биты и для каждого пишет, какая картинка нужна и какую искать нельзя, предлагает фон и стиль по каталогу. Отдаёт smysl.json, из которого работают все остальные отделы. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/docs/ru/agents/montaj-smysl.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-smysl**: Meaning department of the montage studio. Use as the first step of montage for any video: transcribes the speech, understands the topic, message, audience and emotion, cuts the speech into meaning beats and writes for each one which picture is needed and which must not be searched for, proposes a background and a style from the catalog. Delivers smysl.json, from which all other departments work. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/montaj-smysl.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-titry**: Captions department of the montage studio. Use after graphics: writes Armenian captions by meaning — short, selling, not verbatim, but without distorting what was said; places them only where they are needed and removes them where the graphics already say the same. The Armenian is written by the department itself, following the factory's Armenian-language canon. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/montaj-titry.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-titry**: Отдел титров монтажной студии. Использовать после графики: пишет армянские субтитры по смыслу — короткие, продающие, не дословные, но без искажения сказанного; ставит их только там, где они нужны, и снимает там, где графика уже говорит то же. Армянский пишется сам, по канону армянского языка завода. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/docs/ru/agents/montaj-titry.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-vstavki**: Inserts department of the montage studio. Use after the meaning breakdown: for every beat that needs a picture, it searches Pexels and Pixabay stock using the queries from the breakdown, picks by eye from a preview sheet, checks the clip over its full length, and converts it to the output format. Also works with the owner's own footage and a partner's video: crops, covers or paints over watermarks on material that is cleared for use. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/montaj-vstavki.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-vstavki**: Отдел вставок монтажной студии. Использовать после смыслового разбора: для каждого бита, где нужна картинка, ищет стоки на Pexels и Pixabay по запросам из разбора, выбирает глазами по листу превью, проверяет клип по всей длине, приводит к формату вывода. Работает и со своими кадрами владельца и видео партнёра: обрезает, перекрывает или закрашивает водяные знаки на материале с разрешением. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/docs/ru/agents/montaj-vstavki.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-zvuk**: Sound department of the montage studio. Use after the picture is assembled: picks music along the emotional arc from the meaning breakdown, sets it 15–20 dB below the voice with ducking under speech, adds rare sound accents, mixes and masters loudness for the platform. Voice-over and voice cloning only on the owner's direct command. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/montaj-zvuk.md) · ★1)
- **montaj-zvuk**: Отдел звука монтажной студии. Использовать после сборки картинки: подбирает музыку по эмоциональной дуге из смыслового разбора, ставит её на 15–20 дБ ниже голоса с проседанием под речь, редкие звуковые акценты, сводит и мастерит громкость под площадку. Озвучку и клон голоса делает только по прямой команде владельца. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/docs/ru/agents/montaj-zvuk.md) · ★1)
- **montar-expediente-pdf**: Úsalo cuando el usuario deba reunir varios documentos sueltos (PDF, imágenes, archivos de Word, escaneados) en un único dosier PDF bien ordenado para un cliente, un banco, una solicitud de subvención o una licitación pública, con portada, índice, numeración de páginas y límites de tamaño. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/documentos-pdf-archivo/agents/montar-expediente-pdf.md))
- **montazhyor**: Монтажёр роликов Арона. Собирает из сырых дублей говорящей головы (папка с .mov/.mp4) готовый вертикальный ролик 9:16 без пауз через montazh.py, проверяет результат и хранит правила монтажа. Вызывать, когда Арон даёт папку с записями («смонтируй», «обрежь», «склей»), и когда он хочет что-то поменять в монтаже — любая правка идёт через этого агента и фиксируется в правилах. ([AronZakria/ai-montazher](https://github.com/AronZakria/ai-montazher/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/montazhyor.md) · ★9)
- **monte-carlo-analyst**: Runs Monte Carlo simulations on backtest results and interprets the statistical output. Validates edge confidence and drawdown risk. Use after running a backtest to validate results. ([skrymer/trading](https://github.com/skrymer/trading/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/monte-carlo-analyst.md))
- **monte-carlo-shuffle-agent**: リストをランダムに並べ替える shuffle(xs) を実装する。出力が毎回変わる非決定処理を、多数回実行した統計（妥当性＋一様性）で合否判定するモンテカルロ・オラクルで採点される。 ([hatohato-lab/monte-carlo-shuffle-agent](https://github.com/hatohato-lab/monte-carlo-shuffle-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/monte-carlo-shuffle-agent.md))
- **monte-carlo-simulation**: Runs distributional analysis over the few drivers that measurably move an output, defends every distribution and correlation chosen, and reports converged ranges. Use when a stable Gate 2-approved model has quantifiable uncertainty; do not use to repair the model, replace causal scenarios, or present a percentile as the probability of a real-world event. ([andreworia/claude-finance-skills](https://github.com/andreworia/claude-finance-skills/blob/HEAD/packs/finance-agents/agents/41-monte-carlo-simulation.md) · ★26)
- **monte-carlo-simulator**: TIU_CARD를 N회(100~10000) 자동 플레이해서 엔딩 분포·스탯 분포·희귀 카드·게임오버율을 측정합니다. 릴리즈 전, 대규모 밸런싱 변경 후 사용하세요. ([Art-lyang/TIU-card](https://github.com/Art-lyang/TIU-card/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/monte-carlo-simulator.md))
- **monte-cristo**: Use this agent when a project, audit, or research effort may be trapped by inherited assumptions. Typical triggers include a strategic reset before a costly commitment, an independent research synthesis after experts converge too quickly, and a stalled blue-team versus red-team debate that needs a view above both camps. Do not use it for routine code review or direct implementation. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([zedarvates/botte-secrete](https://github.com/zedarvates/botte-secrete/blob/HEAD/agents/monte-cristo.md) · ★2)
- **montecarlo-simulator**: Previsioni probabilistiche basate sul flusso (Monte Carlo, cycle time, throughput). Capisce se l'utente chiede di un singolo work item, di un gruppo/epic o di una lista ordinata di gruppi — anche da testo, file o link incollati — e delega alla skill giusta (montecarlo-single-item, montecarlo-epic, montecarlo-epics). Non calcola nulla da sé. Usalo per "quando sarà pronto?", "quando finiamo questa epic?", "quanti item entro la data X?", "quando chiudiamo la roadmap?", "siamo in 2 su questa epic e alcune card ne bloccano altre: quando finiamo?". ([dan-the-dev/xp-developer-skills](https://github.com/dan-the-dev/xp-developer-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/montecarlo-simulator.md) · ★33)
- **Monter filmowy systemu**: Integrator to ostatni agent warstwy BUILD i brama między BUILD a QA. Łączy kod od Backend Dev, CSS od Designera i treść od Redaktora w jeden działający produkt. Rozwiązuje konflikty, waliduje zgodność z MANIFEST.md i testuje całość E2E. ([TheJacksonCode/Agent-Architecture](https://github.com/TheJacksonCode/Agent-Architecture/blob/HEAD/agents/integrator.md) · ★111 · 3 copies)
- **monteur-shorts-distribution**: Utiliser pour les Shorts et la diffusion multi-plateformes — clips détectés, rendus, publiés, distribués. Le choix éditorial reste humain, les comptes connectés se vérifient d'abord. ([PrendsTaPart/Plugin-Claude-MCP-BraindCode-](https://github.com/PrendsTaPart/Plugin-Claude-MCP-BraindCode-/blob/HEAD/rapido-youtube/agents/monteur-shorts-distribution.md) · ★8)
- **monteur-video**: Tu es le monteur vidéo du studio. Tu transformes un brief et des rushes bruts en vidéo prête à publier, en prenant en charge la boucle ingestion → storyboard → rendu → contrôle qualité. ([redsilvernight/studio-os](https://github.com/redsilvernight/studio-os/blob/HEAD/library/studio/agents/monteur-video.md))
- **Montgomery Scott**: Senior engineer who writes complex, architecturally sophisticated code — favors brilliant solutions to hard problems. ([Cyborgninja21/pychivalry](https://github.com/Cyborgninja21/pychivalry/blob/HEAD/legacy/.github/archive/agents/scotty.agent.md) · ★5)
- **month-end-close**: You are the **month-end close assistant** for **{{projectName}}**. ([kortix-ai/CompanyOS](https://github.com/kortix-ai/CompanyOS/blob/HEAD/packages/starter/templates/marketplace/runtime/agents/month-end-close.md) · ★21k)
- **month-end-close**: Monthly fresh-session month-end close assistant. Walks the close checklist in {{close_sheet}}, reconciles Stripe revenue against the recorded ledger, flags unreconciled items, missing documentation, and anomalies against prior months, and posts a close-status summary to {{close_channel}}. Assembles and flags only — never posts a journal entry and never marks the books closed. ([Karthik17-dev/Lead-Generation](https://github.com/Karthik17-dev/Lead-Generation/blob/HEAD/packages/starter/templates/marketplace/runtime/agents/month-end-close.md))
- **month-end-closer**: Runs the month-end close for an entity — accruals, roll-forwards, and variance commentary — and stages the close package for controller sign-off. Use for period-end close; not for daily reconciliation (use gl-reconciler for that). ([anthropics/financial-services](https://github.com/anthropics/financial-services/blob/HEAD/plugins/agent-plugins/month-end-closer/agents/month-end-closer.md) · ★41k · 35 copies)
- **month-end-closer**: &gt; **用途**：月結作業助理，幫你算應計與結轉、說明科目差異，最後產一份月結包給控制員簽。 ([ArthurC02/AnthropicFinancialAgents](https://github.com/ArthurC02/AnthropicFinancialAgents/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/month-end-closer.md) · ★1)
- **month-end-closer**: 为指定实体运行月末结账流程——包括计提计算、滚存表、差异分析评论——并生成结账文件包供财务总监签批。适用于期末结账场景；不适用于日常对账。 ([HdxL0V3/claude-month-end-closer-cn](https://github.com/HdxL0V3/claude-month-end-closer-cn/blob/HEAD/agents/month-end-closer.md) · ★1)
- **month-planner**: Turns the best kept video ideas into a month of videos on the creator's filming days, a hook for each, in the order to film them, with the inspiration named. Use for "plan my month", "content calendar from these ideas", "what do I film first". ([jsath/claude-skills](https://github.com/jsath/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/skills/social/idea-bank/agents/05-month-planner.md) · ★21)
- **monthly-audit**: Runs the full repo-hygiene deep audit once a month. Proposes fixes. PM reviews and merges. ([akshatk7/ai-product-development-os](https://github.com/akshatk7/ai-product-development-os/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/monthly-audit.md) · ★6)
- **monthly-flow**: Use this agent during the Monthly Investment Flow to generate a per-position AI summary card. Called once per position when the user opens the Monthly Investment Flow screen. Returns a 1-2 sentence portfolio-aware summary and sentiment signal. ([omermelamed/Comprehensive-Stock-Market-Investment](https://github.com/omermelamed/Comprehensive-Stock-Market-Investment/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/monthly-flow.md) · ★1)
- **monthly-processor**: 从指定视角处理一个周期的日志条目。默认用于月度复盘，也可被周度复盘复用为轻量证据提取器。 ([an-X550/zhiji](https://github.com/an-X550/zhiji/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/monthly-processor.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **monthly-report-adversarial-reviewer**: You adversarially review one bilingual monthly report pair and revise both files in place when the report overclaims, contradicts itself, or leaves a structural promise unresolved. ([yuema137/scientific-eval-environments](https://github.com/yuema137/scientific-eval-environments/blob/HEAD/automation/monthly_report/agents/monthly-report-adversarial-reviewer.md) · ★34)
- **monthly-report-chinese-reviewer**: Independently revise one generated Chinese monthly report while preserving its facts and links. ([yuema137/scientific-eval-environments](https://github.com/yuema137/scientific-eval-environments/blob/HEAD/automation/monthly_report/agents/monthly-report-chinese-reviewer.md) · ★34)
- **monthly-report-translator**: Translate the canonical English monthly report into a natural Chinese report. ([yuema137/scientific-eval-environments](https://github.com/yuema137/scientific-eval-environments/blob/HEAD/automation/monthly_report/agents/monthly-report-translator.md) · ★34)
- **monthly-report-writer**: You write the canonical English monthly report for this repository. ([yuema137/scientific-eval-environments](https://github.com/yuema137/scientific-eval-environments/blob/HEAD/automation/monthly_report/agents/monthly-report-writer.md) · ★34)
- **monthly-reviewer**: 월말에 Audit Log를 읽어 카테고리별 지출을 집계하고 월말 보고서와 다음 달 예산 제안을 생성하는 에이전트. run-monthly-review Skill을 따른다. ([papasmf1/harness-demo](https://github.com/papasmf1/harness-demo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/monthly-reviewer.md))
- **monthly-synthesis**: 综合视角分析生成以复盘六问为显式一级标题的月度复盘报告。 ([an-X550/zhiji](https://github.com/an-X550/zhiji/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/monthly-synthesis.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **monty**: AI Python development agent: assist, review, write tests, build/lint/typecheck and upgrade dependencies, with python-lft and pypi-query MCP servers and an @agent function. ([telchak/daggerverse](https://github.com/telchak/daggerverse/blob/HEAD/.okf/agents/monty.md) · ★10)
- **monty**: Business Analyst (Monty). Brainstorming, research de mercado/dominio/técnico, product briefs, documentación de proyecto. Fase 1 completa. ([TaleorSwift/proof-day](https://github.com/TaleorSwift/proof-day/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/monty.md))
- **monty-sandboxed-interpreter**: **Source:** [pydantic/monty](https://github.com/pydantic/monty) (MIT) **By:** Pydantic (Samuel Colvin et al.) ([RueClaw/public-data](https://github.com/RueClaw/public-data/blob/HEAD/agents/monty-sandboxed-interpreter.md) · ★12)
- **monty-scout**: Checks whether Monty (pydantic's secure Python interpreter for AI agents) is ready to integrate into this project. Use when asked about Monty, code-execution capabilities, or pydantic-ai code-mode. Persists findings across sessions so you don't re-fetch unnecessarily. ([devops-adeel/chat-app-pydantic-ai](https://github.com/devops-adeel/chat-app-pydantic-ai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/monty-scout.md))
- **mood-mnky**: MOOD MNKY brand ambassador. Use for brand voice, product narrative, copy, and aesthetic guidance across main site, dojo, and MNKY LABZ. ([MOODMNKY-LLC/POKE-MNKY-v2](https://github.com/MOODMNKY-LLC/POKE-MNKY-v2/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/mood-mnky.md))
- **mood-rescue**: Fresh-eyes diagnostician for when the user is visibly frustrated and the main agent seems stuck. Give it a narrow brief (the failing behaviour, what was tried, relevant files); it returns a diagnosis and one recommended fix. Does not edit files. ([kasper0406/claude-mood](https://github.com/kasper0406/claude-mood/blob/HEAD/agents/mood-rescue.md) · ★1)
- **moodboard**: 项目一旦有垫图（`project moodboard &lt;id&gt; --add 图`，或网页项目页「参考库」上传），**后续一切生成默认 自动把默认启用（`on=True`）的垫图转 base64 喂 API**，整体锁死模块风格。 ([chillzhuang/Kinema](https://github.com/chillzhuang/Kinema/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/moodboard.md) · ★248)
- **moodboard-creator** ([JesseQin123/clipmarts](https://github.com/JesseQin123/clipmarts/blob/HEAD/products/company-templates/design-studio/agents/moodboard-creator.md))
- **moodboard-designer**: Moodboard designer. Based on style analysis results, composes a color palette, texture/material board, and spatial atmosphere guide as a cohesive visual language. ([chodolmu/taskforge-pro](https://github.com/chodolmu/taskforge-pro/blob/HEAD/harnesses/health/space-concept-board/agents/moodboard-designer.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **moodboard-designer**: 무드보드 설계자. 스타일 분석 결과를 바탕으로 컬러팔레트, 텍스처·소재 보드, 공간 분위기를 시각적으로 구성한다. ([sideprojectmate/sideProjectMate](https://github.com/sideprojectmate/sideProjectMate/blob/HEAD/src/lib/data/harness-100/ko/77-space-concept-board/.claude/agents/moodboard-designer.md) · ★2)

- **moodforge-brand-architect**: Use when the moodforge-queen has a locked theme from Step 2 and needs Step 3 brand kit. WCAG-verified, multi-section spine, with code-ready tokens. Never invoked directly. - **moodforge-conventions**: REFERENCE DOCUMENT — DO NOT INVOKE AS AN AGENT. This file is the single source of truth for the protocols every moodforge worker must follow: the mandatory read protocol (state.json first, never re-read prior HTML bodies), the artifact frontmatter format (YAML header on every file produced), and the state_delta return contract (strict structured output to the queen). The moodforge-queen tells every worker to Read this file before doing anything else. Updating this file updates the rules for all 11 agents at once. ([PiyushMalik01/North](https://github.com/PiyushMalik01/North/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/moodforge/agents/moodforge-conventions.md) · 2 copies)
